\"label_match\": 1.0,\n            \"temporal\": 1.0,\n        }\n        \n        # Statistics\n        self._total_adjudications = 0\n        self._confirmed_count = 0\n        self._rejected_count = 0\n    \n    def record_adjudication(\n        self,\n        candidate: VASPCandidate,\n        decision: AttributionStatus,\n        decided_by: str,\n        reason: str,\n        confidence_override: Optional[float] = None,\n    ) -> AdjudicationRecord:\n        \"\"\"Record an adjudication decision.\"\"\"\n        import uuid\n        \n        record = AdjudicationRecord(\n            adjudication_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n            candidate_id=candidate.candidate_id,\n            address=candidate.address,\n            chain=candidate.chain,\n self._candidate_index[candidate.candidate_id] = []\n        self._candidate_index[candidate.candidate_id].append(record.adjudication_id)\n        \n        addr_key = self._address_index[addr_key].append(record.adjudication_id)\n        \n        # Update statistics\n        self._total_adjudications += 1\n        if decision == AttributionStatus.CONFIRMED:\n            self._confirmed_count += 1\n            # Boost weights for confirmed factors\n            self._update_weights(candidate, boost=True)\n        elif decision == AttributionStatus.REJECTED:\n            self._rejected_count += 1\n            # Reduce weights for rejected factors\n            self._update_weights(candidate, boost=False)\n        \n        # Update candidate status\n        candidate.status = decision\n        candidate.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        \n        if confidence_override is not None:\n            candidate.confidence = confidence_override\n        \n        return record\n    \n    def get_adjudication(self, adjudication_id: str) -> Optional[AdjudicationRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Get an adjudication record.\"\"\"\n        return     ids = self._candidate_index.get(candidate_id, [])\n        return [self._records[cid] for cid in ids if cid in self._records]\n    \n    def get_adjudications_for_address(self, address: str,     \"total_adjudications\": self._total_adjudications,\n            \"confirmed_count\": self._confirmed_count,\n            \"rejected_count\": self._rejected_count,\n None:\n        \"\"\"Update feedback weights based on adjudication.\"\"\"\n        adjustment = 0.1 if boost else -0.1\n        \n        for factor in candidate.confidence_factors:\n            current_weight = self._feedback_weights.get(factor.factor_type, 1.0)\n            new_weight = max(0.1, min(2.0, current_weight + adjustment))\n            self._feedback_weights[factor.factor_type] = {}  # addr -> [candidate_ids]\n    \n    def register_known_address(self, entry: KnownAddress) -> KnownAddress:\n        \"\"\"Register a known address in the registry.\"\"\"\n        return self._address_candidates[addr_key].append(candidate.candidate_id)\n        \n        return candidate\n    \n    def get_top_candidates(\n        self,\n        address: str,\n        chain:           \"adjudication\": self.adjudication.get_statistics(),\n            \"total_candidates\": len(self._candidates),\n            \"feedback_weights\": self.adjudication.get_feedback_weights(),\n        }\n"
}

storage_key: Optional[str] = None,\n        metadata: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> EvidenceItem:\n        \"\"\"Add an item to a package.\"\"\"\n        import uuid\n        \n        package = self._packages.get(package_id)\n        if not package:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Package not found: {package_id}\")\n        \n        if package.is_sealed:\n            raise ValueError(\"Cannot add items to a sealed package\")\n        \n        # Calculate content hash\n        content_str = json.dumps(content, sort_keys=True, default=str)\n        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(content_str.encode()).hexdigest()\n        \n        item = EvidenceItem(\n            item_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n            item_type=item_type,\n            content=content,\n            content_hash=content_hash,\n            storage_key=storage_key,\n            description=description,\n            metadata=metadata or {},\n        )\n        \n        package.items.append(item)\n        package.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        \n        # Recalculate package hash\n        package.content_hash = self._calculate_package_hash(package)\n        \n        return item\n    \n    def add_transaction_evidence(\n        self,\n        package_id: str,\n        transaction: NormalizedTransaction,\n        description: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> EvidenceItem:\n        \"\"\"Add a transaction as evidence.\"\"\"\n        content = {\n            \"tx_hash\": transaction.tx_hash,\n            \"chain\": transaction.chain.value if isinstance(transaction.chain, ChainType) else transaction.chain,\n            \"block_number\": transaction.block_number,\n            \"block_timestamp\": transaction.block_timestamp.isoformat(),\n            \"from_address\": transaction.from_address,\n            \"to_address\": transaction.to_address,\n            \"value\": transaction.value,\n            \"currency\": transaction.currency,\n            \"transaction_type\": transaction.transaction_type.value if hasattr(transaction.transaction_type, 'value') else transaction.transaction_type,\n            \"is_success\": transaction.is_success,\n            \"risk_score\": transaction.risk_score,\n            \"is_suspicious\": transaction.is_suspicious,\n        }\n        \n        return self.add_item(\n            package_id,\n            ItemType.TRANSACTION,\n            content,\n            description or f\"Transaction {transaction.tx_hash}\",\n        )\n    \n    def add_block_data(\n        self,\n        package_id: str,\n        block_data: dict[str, Any],\n        chain: ChainType,\n        description: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> EvidenceItem:\n        \"\"\"Add block data as evidence.\"\"\"\n        content = {\n            \"chain\": chain.value,\n            \"block_number\": block_data.get(\"number\"),\n            \"block_hash\": block_data.get(\"hash\"),\n            \"timestamp\": block_data.get(\"timestamp\"),\n            \"transactions\": block_data.get(\"transactions\", 0),\n            \"gas_used\": ItemType.BLOCK_DATA,\n            content,\n            description or f\"Block {block_data.get('number')} on {chain.value}\",\n        )\n    \n    def seal_package(self, package_id: str) -> EvidencePackage:\n        \"\"\"Seal a package (makes it immutable).\"\"\"\n        package = self._packages.get(package_id)\n        if not package:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Package not found: {package_id}\")\n        \n        if package.is_sealed:\n            raise ValueError(\"Package is already sealed\")\n        \n        # Calculate final hash\n        package.content_hash = self._calculate_package_hash(package)\n        \n        # Seal\n        package.is_sealed = True\n        package.sealed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        package.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        \n        # Add to hash chain\n        self._hash_chain.append(package.content_hash)\n        \n        return package\n    \n    def verify_package(self, package_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Verify package integrity.\"\"\"\n        package = self._packages.get(package_id)\n        if not package:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Package not found: {package_id}\")\n        \n        verification_result = {\n            \"package_id\": package_id,\n            \"is_sealed\": package.is_sealed,\n            \"item_count\": len(package.items),\n            \"items_verified\": 0,\n            \"items_failed\": 0,\n            \"package_hash_valid\": False,\n            \"overall_status\": VerificationStatus.UNVERIFIED,\n        }\n        \n        # Verify each item\n        for item in package.items:\n            content_str = json.dumps(item.content, sort_keys=True, default=str)\n            computed_hash = hashlib.sha256(content_str.encode()).hexdigest()\n            \n            if computed_hash == item.content_hash:\n                verification_result[\"items_verified\"] += 1\n            else:\n                verification_result[\"items_failed\"] += 1\n        \n        # Verify package hash\n        computed_package_hash = self._calculate_package_hash(package)\n        verification_result[\"package_hash_valid\"] = computed_package_hash == package.content_hash\n        \n        # Determine overall status\n        if (verification_result[\"items_failed\"] == 0 and \n            verification_result[\"package_hash_valid\"] and\n            package.is_sealed):\n            verification_result[\"overall_status\"] = VerificationStatus.VERIFIED\n            package.verification_status = VerificationStatus.VERIFIED\n            package.verified_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        elif verification_result[\"items_failed\"] > 0 or not verification_result[\"package_hash_valid\"]:\n            verification_result[\"overall_status\"] = VerificationStatus.TAMPERED\n            package.verification_status = VerificationStatus.TAMPERED\n        else:\n            verification_result[\"overall_status\"] = VerificationStatus.UNVERIFIED\n        \n        return verification_result\n    \n    def get_package(self, package_id: str) ->        \"\"\"Get all packages for a case.\"\"\"\n        package_ids = self._case_index.get(case_id, [])\n        return [self._packages[pid] for pid in package_ids if pid in self._packages]\n    \n    def get_packages_for_finding(self, finding_id: str) -> list[EvidencePackage]:\n        \"\"\"Get all packages for a finding.\"\"\"\n        package_ids = self._finding_index.get(finding_id, [])\n        return [self._packages[pid] for pid in package_ids if pid in self._packages]\n    \n    def export_package(\n        self,\n        package_id: str,\n        format: ReportFormat = ReportFormat.JSON,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Export a package in the specified format.\"\"\"\n        package = self._packages.get(package_id)\n        if not package:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Package not found: {package_id}\")\n        \n        if format == ReportFormat.JSON:\n            return self._export_json(package)\n        elif format == ReportFormat.HTML:\n            return self._export_html(package)\n        elif format == ReportFormat.CSV:\n            return self._export_csv(package)\n        else:\n            return self._export_json(package)\n    \n    def get_statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get evidence service statistics.\"\"\"\n        packages = list(self._packages.values())\n        \n        if not packages:\n            return {\"total_packages\": 0}\n        \n        # Count by type\n        by_type = {}\n        for pkg in packages:\n            pkg_type = pkg.package_type.value\n            by_type[pkg_type] = by_type.get(pkg_type, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count by status\n        by_status = {}\n        for pkg in packages:\n            status = pkg.verification_status.value\n            by_status[status] = by_status.get(status, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count sealed vs unsealed\n        sealed_count = sum(1 for pkg in packages if pkg.is_sealed)\n        \n        # Total items\n        total_items = sum(len(pkg.items) for pkg in packages)\n        \n        return {\n            \"total_packages\": len(packages),\n            \"sealed_count\": sealed_count,\n            \"unsealed_count\": len(packages) - sealed_count,\n            \"total_items\": total_items,\n            \"by_type\": by_type,\n            \"by_status\": by_status,\n            \"hash_chain_length\": len(self._hash_chain),\n        }\n    \n    def _calculate_package_hash(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Calculate SHA-256 hash of package content.\"\"\"\n        # Create a deterministic representation\n        content = {\n            \"package_id\": package.package_id,\n            \"case_id\": package.case_id,\n            \"package_type\": package.package_type.value,\n            \"items\": [\n                {\n                    \"item_id\": item.item_id,\n                    \"item_type\": item.item_type.value,\n                    \"content_hash\": item.content_hash,\n                }\n                for item in package.items\n            ],\n            \"created_by\": package.created_by,\n            \"created_at\": package.created_at.isoformat(),\n        }\n        \n        content_str = json.dumps(content, sort_keys=True)\n        return hashlib.sha256(content_str.encode()).hexdigest()\n    \n    def _export_json(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Export package as JSON.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"package_id\": package.package_id,\n            \"case_id\": package.case_id,\n            \"package_type\": package.package_type.value,\n            \"title\": package.title,\n            \"description\": package.description,\n            \"finding_id\": package.finding_id,\n            \"content_hash\": package.content_hash,\n            \"is_sealed\": package.is_sealed,\n            \"sealed_at\": package.sealed_at.isoformat() if package.sealed_at else None,\n            \"verification_status\": package.verification_status.value,\n            \"created_by\": package.created_by,\n            \"created_at\": package.created_at.isoformat(),\n            \"items\": [\n                {\n                    \"item_id\": item.item_id,\n                    \"item_type\": item.item_type.value,\n                    \"content\": item.content,\n                    \"content_hash\": item.content_hash,\n                    \"description\": item.description,\n                    \"created_at\": item.created_at.isoformat(),\n                }\n                for item in package.items\n            ],\n            \"tags\": package.tags,\n            \"metadata\": package.metadata,\n        }\n    \n    def _export_html(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Export package as HTML.\"\"\"\n        html_content = f\"\"\"<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html>\n<head>\n    <title>Evidence Package: {package.package_id}</title>\n    <style>\n        body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 40px; }}\n        .header {{ background: #f5f5f5; padding: 20px;   table {{ border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }}\n        th, td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }}\n        th {{ background: #f5f5f5; }}\n {package.case_id}</p>\n        <p><strong>Type:</strong> {package.package_type.value}</p>\n        <p><strong>Status:</strong> {package.verification_status.value}</p>\n html_content += f\"\"\"\n    <div class=\"item\">\n        <h3>{item.item_type.value}: {item.description or 'No description'}</h3>\n        <p class=\"hash\">Hash: {item.content_hash}</p>\n        <pre>{json.dumps(item.content, indent=2)}</pre>\n    </div>\n\"\"\"\n        \n        html_content += f\"\"\"\n    <div class=\"header\">\n        <h2>Integrity</h2>\n        <p class=\"hash\">Package Hash: {package.content_hash}</p>\n        <p>Created: {package.created_at.isoformat()}</p>\n        <p>Created By: {package.created_by}</p>\n    </div>\n</body>\n</html>\"\"\"\n        \n        return {\n            \"format\": \"html\",\n            \"content\": html_content,\n            \"package_id\": package.package_id,\n        }\n    \n    def _export_csv(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Export package as CSV.\"\"\"\n        csv_rows = [\"item_id,item_type,description,content_hash,created_at\"]\n        \n        for item in package.items:\n            csv_rows.append(\n                f\"{item.item_id},{item.item_type.value},\"\n                f\"\\\"{item.description or ''}\\\",{item.content_hash},\"\n                f\"{item.created_at.isoformat()}\"\n            )\n        \n        return {\n            \"format\": \"csv\",\n            \"content\": \"\\n\".join(csv_rows),\n            \"package_id\": package.package_id,\n        }\n"
}

in events:\n            if event.chain:\n                chain = event.chain.value\n                events_by_chain[chain] = events_by_chain.get(chain, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Value statistics\n        values = [e.value for e in events if e.value is not None]\n        total_value = sum(values)\n        max_value = max(values) if values else 0\n        avg_value = total_value / len(values) if values else 0\n        \n        # Risk statistics\n        suspicious = [e for e in events if e.is_suspicious]\n        risk_scores = [e.risk_score for e in events if e.risk_score is not None]\n        avg_risk = sum(risk_scores) / len(risk_scores) if risk_scores else 0\n        \n        # Unique entities\n        unique_addresses = set()\n        unique_chains = set()\n        for event in events:\n            if event.address:\n                unique_addresses.add(event.address.lower())\n            if event.chain:\n                unique_chains.add(event.chain)\n        \n        return TimelineSummary(\n            total_events=len(events),\n            time_span_hours=round(time_span, 2),\n            first_event=first_event,\n            last_event=last_event,\n            events_by_type=events_by_type,\n            events_by_chain=events_by_chain,\n            total_value=total_value,\n            max_single_value=max_value,\n            avg_value=round(avg_value, 4),\n            suspicious_count=len(suspicious),\n            avg_risk_score=round(avg_risk, 4),\n            unique_addresses=len(unique_addresses),\n            unique_chains=len(unique_chains),\n        )\n    \n    def get_statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get overall timeline statistics.\"\"\"\n        total_events = len(self._events)\n        total_cases = len(self._case_index)\n        \n        # Count by type\n        by_type = {}\n        for event in self._events.values():\n            event_type = event.event_type.value\n            by_type[event_type] = by_type.get(event_type, 0) + 1\n        \n        return {\n            \"total_events\": total_events,\n            \"total_cases\": total_cases,\n            \"events_by_type\": by_type,\n        }\n    \n    def _add_event(self, event: TimelineEvent) -> TimelineEvent:\n        \"\"\"Add an event to the timeline.\"\"\"\n        self._events[event.event_id] = event\n        \n        # Update case index\n        if event.case_id:\n            if event.case_id not in self._case_index:\n                self._case_index[event.case_id] = []\n            self._case_index[event.case_id].append(event.event_id)\n        \n        # Update address index\n        if event.address:\n            addr = event.address.lower()\n            if addr not in self._address_index:\n                self._address_index[addr] = []\n            self._address_index[addr].append(event.event_id)\n        \n        # Update chain index\n        if event.chain:\n            if event.chain not in self._chain_index:\n                self._chain_index[event.chain] = []\n            self._chain_index[event.chain].append(event.event_id)\n        \n        return event\n    \n    def _apply_filter(\n        self,\n        events: list[TimelineEvent],\n        filter: TimelineFilter,\n    ) -> list[TimelineEvent]:\n        \"\"\"Apply filter to events.\"\"\"\n        filtered = events\n        \n        if filter.start_time:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.timestamp >= filter.start_time]\n        \n        if filter.end_time:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.timestamp <= filter.end_time]\n        \n        if filter.event_types:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.event_type in filter.event_types]\n        \n        if filter.chains:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.chain == filter.chains]\n        \n        if filter.addresses:\n            filter_addrs = {a.lower() for a in filter.addresses}\n            filtered = [\n                e for e in filtered\n                if e.address and e.address.lower() in filter_addrs\n            ]\n        \n        if filter.min_value is not None:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.value is not None and e.value >= filter.min_value]\n        \n        if filter.max_value is not None:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.value is not None and e.value <= filter.max_value]\n        \n        if filter.include_suspicious_only:\n            filtered = [e for e in filtered if e.is_suspicious]\n        \n        return filtered\n\n\ndef format_timeline_event(event: TimelineEvent) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format a timeline event for display.\"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        f\"[{event.timestamp.isoformat()}] {event.event_type.value.upper()}\",\n        f\"  {event.title}\",\n    ]\n    \n    if event.description:\n        lines.append(f\"  {event.description}\")\n    \n    if event.tx_hash:\n        lines.append(f\"  Tx: {event.tx_hash}\")\n    \n    if event.address:\n        lines.append(f\"  Address: {event.address}\")\n    \n    if event.chain:\n        lines.append(f\"  Chain: {event.chain.value}\")\n    \n    if event.value is not None:\n        lines.append(f\"  Value: {event.value} {event.currency or ''}\")\n    \n    if event.is_suspicious:\n        lines.append(f\"  ⚠️ SUSPICIOUS (Risk: {event.risk_score:.2f})\")\n    \n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n"
}

Any other relevant information\n\nEvidence should be provided through the secure portal or via encrypted email.\n\nPlease acknowledge this request and provide the requested evidence by the deadline.\n\nThis is an official communication from the CashNet Investigation Platform.\nCase Reference: {case_id}\n\n regards,\nCashNet Investigation Team\"\"\"\n"
}

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "services/integrations/freshness.py",
  "instructions": "Create Data Freshness Monitoring service for blockchain data staleness tracking",
  "content": "\"\"\"Data Freshness Monitoring Service.\n\nMonitors blockchain data freshness, detects staleness, and provides\nalerts for operations dashboard.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta\nfrom enum import Enum\nfrom typing import Any, Optional\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\nfrom ..blockchain.base import ChainType\n\n\nclass FreshnessStatus(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"Data freshness status.\"\"\"\n    FRESH = \"fresh\"\n    ACCEPTABLE = \"acceptable\"\n    STALE = \"stale\"\n    CRITICAL = \"critical\"\n    UNKNOWN = \"unknown\"\n\n\nclass DataSourceType(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"Data source types.\"\"\"\n    BLOCKCHAIN_NODE = \"blockchain_node\"\n    EXPLORER_API = \"explorer_api\"\n    GRAPH_DATABASE = \"graph_database\"\n    CACHE = \"cache\"\n    INDEX = \"index\"\n\n\nclass FreshnessMetric(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Freshness metric for a data source.\"\"\"\n    source_id: str\n    source_type: DataSourceType\n    chain: ChainType\n    \n    # Timestamps\n    last_updated: datetime\n    last_successful_sync: Optional[datetime] = None\n    next_expected_sync: Optional[datetime] = None\n    \n    # Status\n    status: FreshnessStatus = FreshnessStatus.UNKNOWN\n    \n    # Lag metrics\n    lag_seconds: int = 0\n    lag_blocks: int = 0\n    current_block: Optional[int] = None\n    synced_block: Optional[int] = None\n    \n    # Thresholds (in seconds)\n    fresh_threshold: int = 300      # 5 minutes\n    acceptable_threshold: int = 3600  # 1 hour\n    stale_threshold: int = 86400     # 24 hours\n    \n    # Metadata\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n\nclass FreshnessAlert(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Freshness alert.\"\"\"\n    alert_id: str\n    source_id: str\n    chain: ChainType\n    status: FreshnessStatus\n    message: str\n    lag_seconds: int\n    timestamp: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n    acknowledged: bool = False\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n\nclass FreshnessMonitor:\n    \"\"\"Monitors data freshness across blockchain data sources.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self._metrics: dict[str, FreshnessMetric] = {}\n        self._alerts: list[FreshnessAlert] = []\n        self._alert_callbacks: list[Any] = []\n        \n        # Default thresholds per chain\n        self._chain_thresholds: dict[ChainType, dict[str, int]] = {\n            ChainType.ETHEREUM: {\n                \"fresh\": 300,       # 5 min (12s block time)\n                \"acceptable\": 1800,  # 30 min\n                \"stale\": 3600,      # 1 hour\n            },\n            ChainType.BITCOIN: {\n                \"fresh\": 600,       # 10 min (10 min block time)\n                \"acceptable\": 3600,  # 1 hour\n                \"stale\": 7200,      # 2 hours\n            },\n            ChainType.TRON: {\n                \"fresh\": 180,       # 3 min (3s block time)\n                \"acceptable\": 900,   # 15 min\n                \"stale\": 1800,      # 30 min\n            },\n            ChainType.BNB: {\n                \"fresh\": 180,       # 3 min (3s block time)\n                \"acceptable\": 900,   # 15 min\n                \"stale\": 1800,      # 30 min\n            },\n            ChainType.SOLANA: {\n                \"fresh\": 60,        # 1 min (400ms slot time)\n                \"acceptable\": 300,   # 5 min\n                \"stale\": 600,       # 10 min\n            },\n            ChainType.POLYGON: {\n                \"fresh\": 180,       # 3 min (2s block time)\n                \"acceptable\": 900,   # 15 min\n                \"stale\": 1800,      # 30 min\n            },\n        }\n    \n    def register_source(\n        self,\n        source_id: str,\n        source_type: DataSourceType,\n        chain: ChainType,\n        custom_thresholds: Optional[dict[str, int]] = None,\n    ) -> FreshnessMetric:\n        \"\"\"Register a data source for monitoring.\"\"\"\n        thresholds = self._chain_thresholds.get(chain, {})\n        if custom_thresholds:\n            thresholds.update(custom_thresholds)\n        \n        metric = FreshnessMetric(\n            source_id=source_id,\n            source_type=source_type,\n            chain=chain,\n            last_updated=datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n            fresh_threshold=thresholds.get(\"fresh\", 300),\n            acceptable_threshold=thresholds.get(\"acceptable\", 3600),\n            stale_threshold=thresholds.get(\"stale\", 86400),\n        )\n        \n        self._metrics[source_id] = metric\n        return metric\n    \n    def update_source(\n        self,\n        source_id: str,\n        current_block: int,\n        synced_block: Optional[int] = None,\n    ) -> FreshnessMetric:\n        \"\"\"Update data source with latest block info.\"\"\"\n        metric = self._metrics.get(source_id)\n        if not metric:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Source not found: {source_id}\")\n        \n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        metric.last_updated = now\n        metric.last_successful_sync = now\n        metric.current_block = current_block\n        metric.synced_block = synced_block or current_block\n        \n        # Calculate lag\n        metric.lag_blocks = max(0, current_block - metric.synced_block)\n        \n        # Estimate lag in seconds (simplified)\n        if metric.chain == ChainType.ETHEREUM:\n            metric.lag_seconds = metric.lag_blocks * 12\n        elif metric.chain == ChainType.BITCOIN:\n            metric.lag_seconds = metric.lag_blocks * 600\n        elif metric.chain in [ChainType.TRON, ChainType.BNB]:\n            metric.lag_seconds = metric.lag_blocks * 3\n        elif metric.chain == ChainType.SOLANA:\n            metric.lag_seconds = metric.lag_blocks * 1\n        elif metric.chain == ChainType.POLYGON:\n            metric.lag_seconds = metric.lag_blocks * 2\n        else:\n            metric.lag_seconds = metric.lag_blocks * 12  # Default\n        \n        # Determine status\n        metric.status = self._determine_status(metric)\n        \n        # Check for alerts\n        self._check_alerts(metric)\n        \n        return metric\n    \n    def update_source_timestamp(\n        self,\n        source_id: str,\n        last_updated: datetime,\n    ) -> FreshnessMetric:\n        \"\"\"Update data source with timestamp.\"\"\"\n        metric = self._metrics.get(source_id)\n        if not metric:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Source not found: {source_id}\")\n        \n        metric.last_updated = last_updated\n        metric.last_successful_sync = last_updated\n        \n        # Calculate time-based lag\n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        metric.lag_seconds = int((now - last_updated).total_seconds())\n        \n        # Determine status based on time lag\n        if metric.lag_seconds <= metric.fresh_threshold:\n            metric.status = FreshnessStatus.FRESH\n        elif metric.lag_seconds <= metric.acceptable_threshold:\n            metric.status = FreshnessStatus.ACCEPTABLE\n        elif metric.lag_seconds <= metric.stale_threshold:\n            metric.status = FreshnessStatus.STALE\n        else:\n            metric.status = FreshnessStatus.CRITICAL\n        \n        # Check for alerts\n        self._check_alerts(metric)\n        \n        return metric\n    \n    def get_source(self, source_id: str) -> Optional[FreshnessMetric]:\n        \"\"\"Get freshness metric for a source.\"\"\"\n        return self._metrics.get(source_id)\n    \n    def get_chain_overview(self, chain: ChainType) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get freshness overview for a chain.\"\"\"\n        chain_metrics = [\n            m for m in self._metrics.values()\n            if m.chain == chain\n        ]\n        \n        if not chain_metrics:\n            return {\n                \"chain\": chain.value,\n                \"sources\": 0,\n                \"overall_status\": FreshnessStatus.UNKNOWN.value,\n            }\n        \n        # Determine overall status (worst status wins)\n        status_order = {\n            FreshnessStatus.FRESH: 0,\n            FreshnessStatus.ACCEPTABLE: 1,\n            FreshnessStatus.STALE: 2,\n            FreshnessStatus.CRITICAL: 3,\n            FreshnessStatus.UNKNOWN: 4,\n        }\n        \n        worst_status = max(chain_metrics, key=lambda m: status_order.get(m.status, 4))\n        \n        return {\n            \"chain\": chain.value,\n            \"sources\": len(chain_metrics),\n            \"overall_status\": worst_status.status.value,\n            \"max_lag_seconds\": max(m.lag_seconds for m in chain_metrics),\n            \"avg_lag_seconds\": sum(m.lag_seconds for m in chain_metrics) // len(chain_metrics),\n            \"sources_by_status\": {\n                status.value: len([m for m in chain_metrics if m.status == status])\n                for status in FreshnessStatus\n            },\n        }\n    \n    def get_global_overview(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get global freshness overview across all chains.\"\"\"\n        if not self._metrics:\n            return {\"total_sources\": 0, \"chains\": {}}\n        \n        # Group by chain\n        chains = {}\n        for metric in self._metrics.values():\n            chain = metric.chain.value\n            if chain not in chains:\n                chains[chain] = []\n            chains[chain].append(metric)\n        \n        # Calculate overall status\n        all_statuses = [m.status for m in self._metrics.values()]\n        critical_count = all_statuses.count(FreshnessStatus.CRITICAL)\n        stale_count = all_statuses.count(FreshnessStatus.STALE)\n        \n        if critical_count > 0:\n            overall = FreshnessStatus.CRITICAL\n        elif stale_count > 0:\n            overall = FreshnessStatus.STALE\n        elif FreshnessStatus.ACCEPTABLE in all_statuses:\n            overall = FreshnessStatus.ACCEPTABLE\n        else:\n            overall = FreshnessStatus.FRESH\n        \n        return {\n            \"total_sources\": len(self._metrics),\n            \"overall_status\": overall.value,\n            \"chains\": {\n                chain: self.get_chain_overview(ChainType(chain))\n                for chain in chains\n            },\n            \"alerts_count\": len([a for a in self._alerts if not a.acknowledged]),\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n        }\n    \n    def get_alerts(\n        self,\n        chain: Optional[ChainType] = None,\n        status: Optional[FreshnessStatus] = None,\n        unacknowledged_only: bool = False,\n        limit: int = 50,\n    ) -> list[FreshnessAlert]:\n        \"\"\"Get freshness alerts.\"\"\"\n        alerts = self._alerts\n        \n        if chain:\n            alerts = [a for a in alerts if a.chain == chain]\n        \n        if status:\n            alerts = [a for a in alerts if a.status == status]\n        \n        if unacknowledged_only:\n            alerts = [a for a in alerts if not a.acknowledged]\n        \n        # Sort by timestamp descending\n        alerts.sort(key=lambda a: a.timestamp, reverse=True)\n        \n        return alerts[:limit]\n    \n    def acknowledge_alert(self, alert_id: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Acknowledge an alert.\"\"\"\n        for alert in self._alerts:\n            if alert.alert_id == alert_id:\n                alert.acknowledged = True\n                return True\n        return False\n    \n    def get_statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get freshness monitoring statistics.\"\"\"\n        metrics = list(self._metrics.values())\n        \n        if not metrics:\n            return {\"total_sources\": 0}\n        \n        # Count by status\n        by_status = {}\n        for m in metrics:\n            status = m.status.value\n            by_status[status] = by_status.get(status, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count by chain\n        by_chain = {}\n        for m in metrics:\n            chain = m.chain.value\n            by_chain[chain] = by_chain.get(chain, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Lag statistics\n        lag_values = [m.lag_seconds for m in metrics]\n        \n        return {\n            \"total_sources\": len(metrics),\n            \"by_status\": by_status,\n            \"by_chain\": by_chain,\n            \"max_lag_seconds\": max(lag_values),\n            \"avg_lag_seconds\": sum(lag_values) // len(lag_values),\n            \"total_alerts\": len(self._alerts),\n            \"unacknowledged_alerts\": len([a for a in self._alerts if not a.acknowledged]),\n        }\n    \n    def _determine_status(self, metric: FreshnessMetric) -> FreshnessStatus:\n        \"\"\"Determine freshness status based on lag.\"\"\"\n        if metric.lag_seconds <= metric.fresh_threshold:\n            return FreshnessStatus.FRESH\n        elif metric.lag_seconds <= metric.acceptable_threshold:\n            return FreshnessStatus.ACCEPTABLE\n        elif metric.lag_seconds <= metric.stale_threshold:\n            return FreshnessStatus.STALE\n        else:\n            return FreshnessStatus.CRITICAL\n    \n    def _check_alerts(self, metric: FreshnessMetric) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Check if alerts need to be generated.\"\"\"\n        import uuid\n        \n        # Only alert on status changes to STALE or CRITICAL\n        if metric.status in [FreshnessStatus.STALE, FreshnessStatus.CRITICAL]:\n            # Check if we already have an active alert for this source\n            existing_alert = next(\n                (a for a in self._alerts \n                 if a.source_id == metric.source_id and not a.acknowledged),\n                None,\n            )\n            \n            if not existing_alert:\n                alert = FreshnessAlert(\n                    alert_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n                    source_id=metric.source_id,\n                    chain=metric.chain,\n                    status=metric.status,\n                    message=f\"Data source {metric.source_id} is {metric.status.value}: {metric.lag_seconds}s lag\",\n                    lag_seconds=metric.lag_seconds,\n                )\n                \n                self._alerts.append(alert)\n                \n                # Trigger callbacks\n                for callback in self._alert_callbacks:\n                    try:\n                        callback(alert)\n                    except Exception:\n                        pass\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> The issue is that the `export_package` method returns a dict but the verification result is returned from `verify_package`. Let me check the verification result structure - the error says `KeyError: 'format'` which means the test is trying to access `export["format"]` but the export didn't return a dict with 'format' key. Let me look at the verify function again.
> 
> Actually, looking at the test code:
> ```python
> verification = evidence_service.verify_package(package.package_id)
> print(f'Verification status: {verification["overall_status"]}')
> 
> # Export as JSON
> export = evidence_service.export_package(package.package_id)
> print(f'Export format: {export["format"]}')
> ```
> 
> The error is at `export["format"]`. But the `_export_json` method does return `{"format": "html", ...}` for HTML and `{"format": "json", ...}` for JSON.
> 
> Wait, looking at the code, `verify_package` updates `package.verification_status` and then the method returns `verification_result`. But the package is already sealed. Then when we call `export_package`, it's using `ReportFormat.JSON` by default.
> 
> Actually the issue might be that `verify_package` changed the package's `verification_status` to `VERIFIED` but the actual verification status in the return dict shows `VerificationStatus.VERIFIED` (the enum value). But the test was printing the overall_status which worked.
> 
> Wait, the error was `KeyError: 'format'` at line 101. Let me check... the `export_package` method returns `self._export_json(package)` which returns `{"format": "json", ...}`. Hmm, but the test output shows the verification passed, and then there's a `KeyError: 'format'` at line 101. 
> 
> Actually wait - the test output stops at "Verification status: VerificationStatus.VERIFIED" and then errors at line 101. Let me re-examine.
> 
> Looking at the output:
> ```
> Verification status: VerificationStatus.VERIFIED
> ```
> This was printed. Then line 101 should be `export = evidence_service.export_package(package.package_id)` and line 102 should be `print(f'Export format: {export["format"]}')`.
> 
> Hmm but if the package verification_status changed, maybe the _export_json path has an issue. Let me check - the error is `KeyError: 'format'` - meaning the return from `export_package` doesn't have a 'format' key.
> 
> Wait, looking at the export_package method more carefully:
> ```python
> def export_package(self, package_id, format=ReportFormat.JSON):
>     package = self._packages.get(package_id)
>     if not package:
>         raise ValueError(...)
>     if format == ReportFormat.JSON:
>         return self._export_json(package)
>     elif format == ReportFormat.HTML:
>         return self._export_html(package)
>     elif format == ReportFormat.CSV:
>         return self._export_csv(package)
>     else:
>         return self._export_json(package)
> ```
> 
> And `_export_json` returns:
> ```python
> return {
>     "format": "html",  # Wait, that says "html"?!
>     ...
> }
> ```
> 
> Let me check the actual code...
> 
> Looking at _export_html:
> ```python
> return {
>     "format": "html",
>     ...
> }
> ```
> 
> And _export_json:
> ```python
> return {
>     "package_id": package.package_id,
>     ...
> }
> ```
> 
> AH HA! The `_export_json` method doesn't have a "format" key! It returns the data directly without wrapping in a format indicator. Let me look again...
> 
> ```python
> def _export_json(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> dict[str, Any]:
>     return {
>         "package_id": package.package_id,
>         "case_id": package.case_id,
>         ...
>     }
> ```
> 
> No "format" key! The test expects `export["format"]` but the JSON export doesn't include it. Let me fix the test or fix the code. I'll fix the code since the HTML and CSV exports have "format".

The issue is `_export_json` doesn't include a `"format"` key. Let me fix it:

**🛠 Str Replace**

```json
{
  "path": "services/blockchain/evidence.py",
  "replacements": [
    {
      "oldString": "    def _export_json(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Export package as JSON.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"package_id\": package.package_id,",
      "newString": "    def _export_json(self, package: EvidencePackage) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Export package as JSON.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"format\": \"json\",\n            \"package_id\": package.package_id,",

Here's a summary of what I've implemented for Phase 2 and Phase 3:

## Completed Work

### Phase 2 - Core Attribution MVP (New Services)

1. **VASP Attribution Service** (`services/blockchain/attribution.py`)
   - `VersionedRegistry` - Versioned known-address/cluster registry with history tracking
   - `ConfidenceScorer` - Multi-factor confidence scoring (address match, cluster proximity, behavioral, label, temporal)
   - `AdjudicationEngine` - Feedback loop for confirming/rejecting attributions with weight adjustments
   - `VASPAttributionService` - Main service combining all components

2. **Evidence Package Service** (`services/blockchain/evidence.py`)
   - Immutable evidence packages with SHA-256 content hashing
   - Package sealing (makes packages immutable)
   - Integrity verification with hash chain
   - Export to JSON, HTML, and CSV formats

3. **Timeline Generation** (`services/blockchain/timeline.py`)
   - Chronological event timeline from transactions, discoveries, findings
   - Filtering by time, type, chain, address, value
   - Summary statistics (events, values, risk scores)

### Phase 3 - Integrations (New Services)

4. **Financial Institution Notification** (`services/integrations/notification.py`)
   - Fraud alerts, freeze requests, investigation updates, evidence requests
   - Multi-channel delivery (email, SMS, API, webhook)
   - Institution registration and preferences
   - Delivery tracking and retry logic

5. **Data Freshness Monitoring** (`services/integrations/freshness.py`)
   - Chain-aware freshness thresholds (Ethereum 5min, Bitcoin 10min, etc.)
   - Block-based and time-based lag tracking
   - Alert generation for stale/critical data
   - Global and per-chain overview dashboards

### Updated Files
- `services/blockchain/__init__.py` - Added exports for new services
- `services/integrations/__init__.py` - Added exports for new services
- `services/blockchain/ethereum.py` - Fixed web3 middleware import compatibility

  "path": "services/ml/typology.py",

__future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom enum import Enum\nfrom typing import Any, Optional\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\n\nclass TypologyCategory(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"Fraud typology categories.\"\"\"\n    RANSOMWARE = \"ransomware\"\n    PHISHING = \"phishing\"\n    INVESTMENT_SCAM = \"investment_scam\"\n    ROMANCE_SCAM = \"romance_scam\"\n    MONEY_LAUNDERING = \"money_laundering\"\n    TERRORISM_FINANCING = \"terrorism_financing\"\n    SANCTIONS_EVASION = \"sanctions_evasion\"\n    MIXER_TUMBLER = \"mixer_tumbler\"\n    DARKNET_MARKET = \"darknet_market\"\n    SCAM_TOKEN = \"scam_token\"\n    DECENTRALIZED_FINANCE_ABUSE = \"defi_abuse\"\n    NFT_FRAUD = \"nft_fraud\"\n    PIG_BUTCHERING = \"pig_butchering\"\n    OTHER = \"other\"\n\n\nclass MatchSeverity(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"Match severity levels.\"\"\"\n    LOW = \"low\"\n    MEDIUM = \"medium\"\n    HIGH = \"high\"\n    CRITICAL = \"critical\"\n\n\nclass # Multiplier for additional conditions\n    \n    # Metadata\n    version: int = 1\n    is_active: bool = True\n    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n    updated_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n    source: str = \"manual\"  # \"manual\", \"ml_generated\", \"community\"\n    tags: list[str] = []\n    \n    # \"\"\"A detected typology match.\"\"\"\n    match_id: str\n    rule_id: str\n    rule_name: str\n    category: TypologyCategory\n    severity: MatchSeverity\n    \n    # Match details\n    confidence: float\n    matched_conditions: list[str]  # List of matched condition descriptions\n    evidence: list[dict[str, Any]]  # Supporting evidence\n    \n    # Context\n    transaction_hash: Optional[str] datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n    model_version: Optional[str] = None\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n\nclass TypologyEngine:\n    \"\"\"Main typology detection engine.\"\"\"\n    \n    def description=\"3+ similar transactions from same source\",\n                    ),\n                ],\n                base_score=0.7,\n                min_match_count=2,\n                tags=[\"structuring\", \"smurfing\", \"threshold\"],\n            ),\n            TypologyRule(\n                rule_id=\"scam_token_pattern\",\n                name=\"Scam Token Pattern\",\n    ],\n                base_score=0.9,\n                tags=[\"scam\", \"honeypot\", \"token\"],\n            ),\n            TypologyRule(\n                rule_id=\"darknet_market\",\n                    ),\n                ],\n                base_score=0.95,\n                tags=[\"darknet\", \"illicit\", \"marketplace\"],\n            ),\n            TypologyRule(\n                rule_id=\"investment_scam_velocity\",\n                name=\"Investment Scam Velocity\",\n                description=\"Rapid incoming funds from multiple sources (potential rug pull)\",\n                category=TypologyCategory.INVESTMENT_SCAM,\n                severity=MatchSeverity.HIGH,\n                conditions=[\n                    RuleCondition(\n description=\"10+ unique senders in 24 hours\",\n                    ),\n                    RuleCondition(\n                        condition_type=RuleConditionType.TIME_WINDOW,\n                        field=\"first_to_last_transfer\",\n                        operator=\"lt\",\n                        value=86400,  # 24 hours\n                        description=\"All transfers within 24 hour    ),\n            TypologyRule(\n                rule_id=\"sanctions_evasion\",\n                name=\"Sanctions Evasion Pattern\",\n                description=\"Transaction patterns consistent tags=[\"sanctions\", \"OFAC\", \"compliance\"],\n            ),\n            TypologyRule(\n                rule_id=\"bridge_laundering\",\n                name=\"Cross-Chain Laundering via               conditions=[\n                    RuleCondition(\n                        condition_type=RuleConditionType.PATTERN_MATCH,\n                        field=\"transaction_type\",\n                        operator=\"eq\",\n                        value=\"bridge\",\n                        description=\"Bridge transaction detected\",\n                    ),\n                    RuleCondition(\n                        condition_type=RuleConditionType.RISK_SCORE,\n                        field=\"source_risk_score\",\n                        operator=\"gt\",\n min_match_count=2,\n                tags=[\"bridge\", \"cross_chain\", \"layering\"],\n            ),\n        ]\n        \n        for rule in default_rules:\n            self._rules[rule.rule_id] = rule\n            if rule.category not in self._rule_index:\n                self._rule_index[rule.category] = []\n            self._rule_index[rule.category].append(rule.rule_id)\n    \n    def add_rule(self, rule: TypologyRule) -> TypologyRule:\n        \"\"\"Add or update a typology rule.\"\"\"\n        self._rules[rule.rule_id] = rule\n        \n        if rule.category not in self._rule_index:\n            self._rule_index[rule.category] = []\n        if rule.rule_id not in self._rule_index[rule.category]:\n            self._rule_index[rule.category].append(rule.rule_id)\n        \n        return rule\n    \n    def get_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> Optional[TypologyRule]:\n        \"\"\"Get a rule by ID.\"\"\"\n        return self._rules.get(rule_id)\n    \n    def remove_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Remove a rule.\"\"\"\n        rule = self._rules.pop(rule_id, None)\n        if rule:\n            if rule.category in self._rule_index:\n                self._rule_index[rule.category] = [\n                    r for r in self._rule_index[rule.category] if r != rule_id\n                ]\n            return True\n        return False\n    \n    def get_rules_by_category(self, category: TypologyCategory) -> list[TypologyRule]:\n        \"\"\"Get all rules for a category.\"\"\"\n        rule_ids = self._rule_index.get(category, [])\n        return [self._rules[rid] for rid in rule_ids if rid in self._rules]\n    \n    def get_all_active_rules(self) -> list[TypologyRule]:\n        \"\"\"Get all active rules.\"\"\"\n        return [r for r in self._rules.values() if r.is_active]\n    \n    def evaluate_transaction(\n        self,\n        transaction: dict[str, Any],\n        known_addresses: Optional[dict[str, set[str]]] = None,\n        context: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> list[TypologyMatch]:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate a transaction against all active rules.\"\"\"\n        matches: list[TypologyMatch] = []\n        context = context or {}\n        \n        for rule in self.get_all_active_rules():\n            match = self._evaluate_rule(rule, transaction, known_addresses, context)\n            if match:\n                matches.append(match)\n                self._matches.append(match)\n        \n        return matches\n    \n    def evaluate_address(\n        self,\n        address: str,\n        chain: str,\n        address_data: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,\n        known_addresses: Optional[dict[str, set[str]]] = None,\n    ) -> list[TypologyMatch]:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate an address against all active rules.\"\"\"\n        matches: list[TypologyMatch] = []\n        address_data = address_data or {}\n        \n        for rule in self.get_all_active_rules():\n            match = self._evaluate_address_rule(rule, address, chain, address_data, known_addresses)\n            if match:\n                matches.append(match)\n                self._matches.append(match)\n        \n        return matches\n    \n    def get_matches(\n        self,\n        category: Optional[TypologyCategory] = None,\n        severity: Optional[MatchSeverity] = None,\n        case_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        limit: int = 100,\n    ) -> list[TypologyMatch]:\n        \"\"\"Get detection matches with optional filters.\"\"\"\n        results = self._matches\n        \n        if category:\n            results = [m for m in results if m.category == category]\n        if severity:\n            results = [m for m in results if m.severity == severity]\n        if case_id:\n            results = [m for m in results if m.case_id == case_id]\n        \n        return results[:limit]\n    \n    def get_statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get typology detection statistics.\"\"\"\n        rules = list(self._rules.values())\n        matches = self._matches\n        \n        # Count rules by category\n        rules_by_category = {}\n        for rule in rules:\n            cat = rule.category.value\n            rules_by_category[cat] = rules_by_category.get(cat, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count matches by category\n        matches_by_category = {}\n        for match in matches:\n            cat = match.category.value\n            matches_by_category[cat] = matches_by_category.get(cat, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count matches by severity\n        matches_by_severity = {}\n        for match in matches:\n            sev = match.severity.value\n            matches_by_severity[sev] = matches_by_severity.get(sev, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Average confidence\n        avg_confidence = (\n            sum(m.confidence for m in matches) / len(matches)\n            if matches else 0.0\n        )\n        \n        return {\n            \"total_rules\": len(rules),\n            \"active_rules\": sum(1 for r in rules if r.is_active),\n            \"total_matches\": len(matches),\n            \"rules_by_category\": rules_by_category,\n            \"matches_by_category\": matches_by_category,\n            \"matches_by_severity\": matches_by_severity,\n            \"average_confidence\": round(avg_confidence, 4),\n        }\n    \n    def _evaluate_rule(\n        self,\n        rule: TypologyRule,\n        transaction: dict[str, Any],\n        known_addresses: Optional[dict[str, set[str]]],\n        context: dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> Optional[TypologyMatch]:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate a single rule against a transaction.\"\"\"\n        matched_conditions: list[str] = []\n        evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        \n        for condition in rule.conditions:\n            if self._evaluate_condition(condition, transaction, known_addresses, context):\n                matched_conditions.append(\n                    condition.description or f\"{condition.field} {condition.operator} {condition.value}\"\n                )\n                evidence.append({\n                    \"condition_type\": condition.condition_type.value,\n                    \"field\": condition.field,\n                    \"operator\": condition.operator,\n                    \"value\": condition.value,\n                    \"actual_value\": transaction.get(condition.field),\n                })\n        \n        # Check if enough conditions matched\n        if len(matched_conditions) >= rule.min_match_count:\n            # Calculate confidence\n            match_ratio = len(matched_conditions) / len(rule.conditions)\n            confidence = min(\n                rule.base_score * rule.score_multiplier * match_ratio + rule.confidence_boost,\n                1.0,\n            )\n            \n            import uuid\n            return TypologyMatch(\n                match_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n                rule_id=rule.rule_id,\n                rule_name=rule.name,\n                category=rule.category,\n                severity=rule.severity,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                matched_conditions=matched_conditions,\n                evidence=evidence,\n                transaction_hash=transaction.get(\"tx_hash\"),\n                address=transaction.get(\"from_address\") or transaction.get(\"address\"),\n                chain=transaction.get(\"chain\"),\n                case_id=context.get(\"case_id\"),\n            )\n        \n        return None\n    \n    def _evaluate_address_rule(\n        self,\n        rule: TypologyRule,\n        address: str,\n        chain: str,\n        address_data: dict[str, Any],\n        known_addresses: Optional[dict[str, set[str]]],\n    ) -> Optional[TypologyMatch]:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate a single rule against an address.\"\"\"\n        matched_conditions: list[str] = []\n        evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        \n        for condition in rule.conditions:\n            if self._evaluate_condition(condition, {\n                \"address\": address,\n                \"chain\": chain,\n                **address_data,\n            }, known_addresses, {}):\n                matched_conditions.append(\n                    condition.description or f\"{condition.field} {condition.operator}\"\n                )\n                evidence.append({\n                    \"condition_type\": condition.condition_type.value,\n                    \"field\": condition.field,\n                    \"address\": address,\n                    \"chain\": chain,\n                })\n        \n        if len(matched_conditions) >= rule.min_match_count:\n            match_ratio = len(matched_conditions) / len(rule.conditions)\n            confidence = min(\n                rule.base_score * rule.score_multiplier * match_ratio + rule.confidence_boost,\n                1.0,\n            )\n            \n            import uuid\n            return TypologyMatch(\n                match_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n                rule_id=rule.rule_id,\n                rule_name=rule.name,\n                category=rule.category,\n                severity=rule.severity,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                matched_conditions=matched_conditions,\n                evidence=evidence,\n                address=address,\n                chain=chain,\n            )\n        \n        return None\n    \n    def _evaluate_condition(\n        self,\n        condition: RuleCondition,\n        data: dict[str, Any],\n        known_addresses: Optional[dict[str, set[str]]],\n        context: dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate a single condition.\"\"\"\n        actual_value = data.get(condition.field)\n        \n        if actual_value is None:\n            return False\n        \n        try:\n            if condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.VALUE_THRESHOLD:\n                if condition.operator == \"gt\":\n                    return float(actual_value) > float(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"lt\":\n                    return float(actual_value) < float(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"gte\":\n                    return float(actual_value) >= float(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"lte\":\n                    return float(actual_value) <= float(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"eq\":\n                    return float(actual_value) == float(condition.value)\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.VALUE_RANGE:\n                min_val, max_val = condition.value\n                return float(min_val) <= float(actual_value) <= float(max_val)\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.CURRENCY_MATCH:\n                if condition.operator == \"eq\":\n                    return actual_value == condition.value\n                elif condition.operator == \"in\":\n                    return actual_value in condition.value\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.CHAIN_MATCH:\n                return actual_value == condition.value\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.ADDRESS_LIST:\n                if known_addresses and condition.value in known_addresses:\n                    return actual_value.lower() in {a.lower() for a in known_addresses[condition.value]}\n                return False\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.PATTERN_MATCH:\n                if condition.operator == \"eq\":\n                    return actual_value == condition.value\n                elif condition.operator == \"contains\":\n                    return condition.value in str(actual_value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"regex\":\n                    import re\n                    return bool(re.search(condition.value, str(actual_value)))\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.FREQUENCY:\n                if condition.operator == \"gte\":\n                    return int(actual_value) >= int(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"lte\":\n                    return int(actual_value) <= int(condition.value)\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.TIME_WINDOW:\n                if condition.operator == \"lt\":\n                    return float(actual_value) < float(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"gt\":\n                    return float(actual_value) > float(condition.value)\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.COUNTERPARTY_TYPE:\n                return actual_value == condition.value\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.RISK_SCORE:\n                if condition.operator == \"gt\":\n                    return float(actual_value) > float(condition.value)\n                elif condition.operator == \"lt\":\n                    return float(actual_value) < float(condition.value)\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.LABEL_MATCH:\n                if condition.operator == \"contains\":\n                    labels = actual_value if isinstance(actual_value, list) else [actual_value]\n                    target = condition.value if isinstance(condition.value, list) else [condition.value]\n                    return any(label in labels for label in target)\n            \n            elif condition.condition_type == RuleConditionType.VELOCITY:\n                if condition.operator == \"gte\":\n                    return int(actual_value) >= int(condition.value)\n        \n        except (ValueError, TypeError):\n            return False\n        \n        return False\n\n\ndef format_typology_match(match: TypologyMatch) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format a typology match for display.\"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        f\"Typology Match: {match.rule_name}\",\n        f\"Category: {match.category.value}\",\n        f\"Severity: {match.severity.value}\",\n        f\"Confidence: {match.confidence:.1%}\",\n        f\"\",\n        f\"Matched Conditions:\",\n    ]\n    \n    for condition in match.matched_conditions:\n        lines.append(f\"  - {condition}\")\n    \n    if match.address:\n        lines.append(f\"\\nAddress: {match.address}\")\n    if match.chain:\n        lines.append(f\"Chain: {match.chain}\")\n    if match.transaction_hash:\n        lines.append(f\"Transaction: {match.transaction_hash}\")\n    if match.case_id:\n        lines.append(f\"Case: {match.case_id}\")\n    \n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n"
}
```

with governance.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self._models: dict[str, ModelVersion] = {}  # model_id -> ModelVersion\n        self._name_index: dict[str, list[str]] = {}  # model_name      \"\"\"Get a model by ID.\"\"\"\n        return self._models.get(model_id)\n    \n    def get_model_versions(self, model_name: str) -> list[ModelVersion]:\n        \"\"\"Get all versions of a model_name: str) -> Optional[ModelVersion]:\n        \"\"\"Get the latest version of a model.\"\"\"\n        versions = self.get_model_versions(model_name)\n        if not versions:\n            return None\n        \n        # Sort by version (simple string sort for semver)\n        versions.sort(key=lambda m: m.version, reverse=True)\n        return versions[0]\n    \n    def    deployed = [v for v in versions if v.status == ModelStatus.DEPLOYED]\n        return deployed[0] if deployed else None\n    \n    def submit_for_review(self, model_id: str, submitted_by: str) -> ModelVersion:\n        \"\"\"Submit a model for review.\"\"\"\n        model = self._models.get(model_id)\n        if not model:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Model not found: {model_id}\")\n        \n        if model.status != ModelStatus.DRAFT:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Model must be in DRAFT status, got: {model.status}\")\n        \n        model.status = ModelStatus.PENDING_REVIEW\n        model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        \n        model.audit_trail.append({\n            \"action\": \"submitted_for_review\",\n            \"actor\": submitted_by,\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n        })\n        \n        return model\n    \n    def approve_model(\n        self,\n        model_id: str,\n        reviewer_id: str,\n        reviewer_role: str,\n        decision: str,\n        comments: Optional[str] = None,\n        checklist: Optional[dict[str, bool]] = None,\n    ) -> ModelVersion:\n        \"\"\"Approve or reject a model.\"\"\"\n        model = self._models.get(model_id)\n        if not model:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Model not found: {model_id}\")\n        \n        if model.status not in [ModelStatus.PENDING_REVIEW, ModelStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL]:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Model not in review status, got: {model.status}\")\n        \n        import uuid\n        \n        # \"rejected\":\n            model.status = ModelStatus.REJECTED\n        elif decision == \"changes_requested\":\n            model.status = ModelStatus.DRAFT\n        \n        model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        \n        model.audit_trail.append({\n            \"action\": f\"review_{decision}\",\n            \"actor\": reviewer_id,\n            \"role\": reviewer_role,\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            \"comments\": comments,\n        })\n        \n        return model\n    \n    def deploy_model(\n        self,\n        model_id: str,\n        stage: DeploymentStage,\n        deployed_by: str,\n        endpoint_url: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> ModelVersion:\n        \"\"\"Deploy a model to a stage.\"\"\"\n        model = self._models.get(model_id)\n        if not model:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Model not found: {model_id}\")\n        \n        if model.status != ModelStatus.APPROVED and stage ==       model_type: Optional[ModelType] = None,\n        status: Optional[ModelStatus] = None,\n        tag: Optional[str] = None,\n        use_case: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> \"\"\"Get registry statistics.\"\"\"\n        models = list(self._models.values())\n        \n        if not models:\n            return {\"total_models\": 0}\n        \n        # Count by status\n        by_status = {}\n        for m in models:\n            status = m.status.value\n            by_status[status] = by_status.get(status, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count by type\n        by_type = {}\n        for m in models:\n            mtype = m.model_type.value\n            by_type[mtype] = by_type.get(mtype, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Count by deployment stage\n        by_stage = {}\n        for m in models:\n            if m.deployment_stage:\n                stage = m.deployment_stage.value\n                by_stage[stage] = by_stage.get(stage, 0) + 1\n        \n        # Unique model names\n        unique_names = set(m.model_name for m in models)\n        \n        return {\n            \"total_models\": len(models),\n            \"unique_model_names\": len(unique_names),\n            \"by_status\": by_status,\n            \"by_type\": by_type,\n            \"by_deployment_stage\": by_stage,\n            \"deployed_count\": by_status.get(\"deployed\", 0),\n            \"pending_review_count\": by_status.get(\"pending_review\", 0) + by_status.get(\"pending_approval\", 0),\n        }\n    \n    def get_audit_trail(self, model_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Get audit trail for a model.\"\"\"\n        model = self._models.get(model_id)\n        if not model:\n            return []\n        \n        return model.audit_trail\n"
